# Pre-Training

How base models are trained: scaling laws, compute-optimal training, architectures at scale, and the training runs that define each generation.

### Scaling laws & compute-optimal training

How capability scales with model size, data, and compute — Chinchilla-style compute-optimal training and its limits.

### Safety, privacy & robustness

The risks of pretraining at scale: safety, privacy, poisoning, jailbreaks, and hallucinations.

| Agentic Backdoors via Pretraining Poisoning | ★★★★☆ | [2610.37741](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37741) | Xinyan Hu et al. | 2026-10-27 | 2026-11-24 | First end-to-end demonstration that poisoning pretraining data can plant a remote code execution backdoor in an agent: poisoning only 0.1% of the pretraining corpus makes a CLI agent execute attacker-chosen commands on the user's machine when the trigger is present, while behaving normally on clean inputs. Pretrained Qwen3 models from scratch (0.6B, 1.7B, 4B) then post-trained with a clean SFT+DPO+GRPO pipeline; the 4B model reaches ~92% attack success rate at 0.1% poison and stays positive even at 0.0005%; ASR grows with model scale. |
